❓ New here? How to use this page
This shows watches people are trying to buy, with the most recently asked at the top — so you always see the latest interest.
The list shows each watch: how many people asked, your buy price (Your low), and the online resale price (Chrono24). A green ✅ IN STOCK means you own one — go sell it. The gold Sourcing Opportunities box lists in-demand watches you don't stock yet. Below that, click any watch to read the exact messages from people who want it.
📖 Watch words — what the dealer shorthand means
WTB / NTQ / ISO / "looking for"someone who wants to BUY that watch
Slidera watch that looks brand-new (barely worn)
Full set / completecomes with the original box and papers
N5, N7… (or "new 2026")a brand-new 2026 watch — the number is the month
Card / warrantythe official Rolex purchase paper (proves it is real)
MOPmother-of-pearl — a shiny white dial
Oyster / Jubileetwo different Rolex bracelet (strap) styles
PM / DM memessage me privately
HKDHong Kong dollars (÷ ~7.84 for US dollars)
Chrono24a website that shows what watches sell for online
